Must be executable and idempotent (timeout: 720s).\n   - Runs `terraform` or `tofu` against `/workspace/submission/infra` with state saved at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n   - Idempotently initializes the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema, tables, relational constraints, triggers, and indexes on the RDS instance as specified in [RDS PostgreSQL](services/rds.md).\n   - Writes `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (max 1 MiB) conforming strictly to `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`.\n   - Polls `GET <service_url>/health/ready` until the API returns HTTP `200`.\n   - Re-running `deploy.sh` must preserve the RDS instance, DynamoDB table, and S3 bucket without data loss, reconcile control-plane drift across all managed AWS resources (including SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge Scheduler schedules, CloudWatch Log Group retention, Lambda worker environment variables, out-of-band inline or attached IAM policies on the six workload roles, and security group egress rules), recreate any missing resources or event source mappings, and converge all derived data stores (DynamoDB, Valkey, and the versioned S3 audit bucket including purging noncurrent object `Versions` and `DeleteMarkers`) 1-to-1 with authoritative PostgreSQL state before exiting `0`.\n2. `/workspace/submission/destroy.sh`:\n   - Must be executable (timeout: 900s) and cleanly destroy all resources created for `resource_prefix` while leaving all pre-existing baseline (`cl-base-*`) resources untouched.\n   - Must handle non-empty versioned S3 buckets, attached or multi-version IAM policies, and any out-of-band resources scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag) so that post-destroy inventory matches the pre-deployment baseline.\n\n# ClearLedger Runtime and Application Contract\n\nAll four application images are pre-built and loaded into the local container daemon. Do not rebuild or modify the application binaries. Configure each runtime using the environment variables below.\n\n## 1. API Service (`api_image` on ECS Fargate)\n\n- **Binary**: `/usr/local/bin/clearledger-api`\n- **Listening port**: `8080` (`PORT=8080`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `PORT`: `\"8080\"`\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: control-plane endpoint reachable from the container (`http://aws:4566`)\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: Valkey connection URL `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`: `\"90\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"90\"`)\n  - `AUTH_ISSUER`: Cognito issuer URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>`)\n  - `AUTH_AUDIENCES`: comma-separated Cognito client IDs (`<read_client_id>,<write_client_id>,<admin_client_id>`)\n  - `AUTH_JWKS_URL`: Cognito JWKS URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>/.well-known/jwks.json`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the API CloudWatch Log Group\n  - `SERVICE_INSTANCE_ID` (optional): instance identifier returned in `X-ClearLedger-Instance` (defaults to `HOSTNAME`)\n\nUpon startup, `clearledger-api` connects to PostgreSQL and serves HTTP traffic on `0.0.0.0:8080`. The application binaries do **not** run database migrations automatically: `deploy.sh` must idempotently apply the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`, `clearledger.idempotency_keys`), all required relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, all state-transition, cross-table, and append-only triggers across all four tables, and its three indexes as defined in `services/rds.md` before `GET /health/ready` reports `200 OK` (`checks.postgres = \"UP\"`). During `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `clearledger-api` maps PostgreSQL constraint and trigger exceptions (`SQLSTATE 23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001`) to HTTP `400 Bad Request` (`invalid_settlement`).\n\n## 2. Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n\nFor each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n- Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write.\n- Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n- Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`.\n- If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n\n## 3. Outbox Relay Worker (`relay_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 1 minute (`rate(1 minute)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`: `\"50\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"50\"`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the relay CloudWatch Log Group\n\nQueries up to `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE` rows from `clearledger.outbox` where `published_at IS NULL` ordered by `seq ASC`, sends each event envelope to `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, and updates `published_at = NOW(), attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = NULL`.\n\n## 4. No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys, and every role must explicitly deny (`Effect = \"Deny\"`) `kms:DisableKey` and `kms:ScheduleKeyDeletion` on all four customer-managed KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`).\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. ENABLE TRIGGER ALL`).\n\n### 1. Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE`/`BEFORE DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Canonical trimmed strings and length bounds: `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, and `current_stage` (plus `last_memo` when non-null) must be stored in canonical trimmed form (`col = btrim(col)`, no leading or trailing whitespace) and match the length bounds and allowed status values of `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with `debit_party <> credit_party` and `version >= 1`.\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `current_stage = 'INITIATED@' || debit_party`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, `last_memo = 'Settlement initiated'`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`, and `updated_at > created_at`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`) and a strictly increasing `updated_at` timestamp (`NEW.updated_at > OLD.updated_at`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n  - `clearledger.settlements` is an append-only ledger aggregate root (`DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with canonical trimmed storage (`col = btrim(col)`) and length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null canonically trimmed `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` canonically trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` with `data.clearingStage = 'INITIATED@' || (data.debitParty)` and `data.memo = 'Settlement initiated'` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be strictly greater (`>`) than the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`, with canonical trimmed `correlation_id` (`correlation_id = btrim(correlation_id)`).\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `attempts = 0`, both `published_at` and `last_error` must be `NULL`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; when `last_error IS NOT NULL`, `published_at IS NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `length(btrim(last_error)) > 0`, and `last_error = btrim(last_error)`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; on `UPDATE`, envelope columns are immutable, `attempts` cannot decrease, publishing an unpublished row requires incrementing `attempts` with `archived_at IS NULL`, while `published_at` remains `NOT NULL` (such as during archival or resetting `archived_at = NULL` for S3 re-archival), `published_at`, `attempts`, and `last_error` cannot be mutated (resetting `published_at = NULL, archived_at = NULL` for operational replay is permitted), and once `OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL` and `NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL`, `NEW.archived_at` cannot be mutated to a different timestamp without first resetting `archived_at = NULL`.\n\n### 4. Table `clearledger.idempotency_keys`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `scope TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `request_hash TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `status_code INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `response_body JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - Composite `PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - `scope` (`create:<settlement_uuid>` or `entry:<settlement_uuid>`), canonically trimmed `idempotency_key` (`idempotency_key = btrim(idempotency_key)` and `8..128` chars), lowercase hex SHA-256 `request_hash`, `status_code` (`201` for `create:*` at `version = 1`; `202` for `entry:*` at `version >= 2`), and closed-schema `response_body` (`WriteAcceptedResponse` in `openapi.yaml` with `accepted = true` and `idempotentReplay = false`).\n  - On `INSERT`, the referenced event (`eventId`, `settlementId`, `version`, `idempotency_key`) must exist in both `clearledger.events` and `clearledger.outbox`, and each `response_body->>'eventId'` (as well as each `(response_body->>'settlementId', (response_body->>'version')::integer)` pair) must be unique across `clearledger.idempotency_keys`. `UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected.\n\n### 5. Required Indexes in `clearledger` Schema\n\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version`: on `clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version)`\n","metadata":{"output":"# IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies (`services/iam.md`)\n\nProvision six distinct IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with role-specific inline or attached policies (`aws_iam_role_policy` or `aws_iam_policy` + `aws_iam_role_policy_attachment`).\n\n## Global Least-Privilege Rules\n\n- Tag each of the six IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` and `ClearLedgerRole = <ecs_execution|ecs_task|projector|relay|archiver|scheduler>`.\n- Never use wildcard actions (`Action = \"*\"` or `service:*`) or wildcard resources (`Resource = \"*\"`) in any `Allow` statement across any role policy.\n- **Per-workload CloudWatch Log Group isolation**: Each logging workload (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`) may only grant CloudWatch Logs write permissions (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) on its **own** dedicated CloudWatch Log Group ARN (`arn:aws:logs:<region>:<account>:log-group:/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/<workload>*`). No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys, and every role must explicitly deny (`Effect = \"Deny\"`) `kms:DisableKey` and `kms:ScheduleKeyDeletion` on all four customer-managed KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`).\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. ENABLE TRIGGER ALL`).\n\n### 1. Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE`/`BEFORE DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Canonical trimmed strings and length bounds: `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, and `current_stage` (plus `last_memo` when non-null) must be stored in canonical trimmed form (`col = btrim(col)`, no leading or trailing whitespace) and match the length bounds and allowed status values of `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with `debit_party <> credit_party` and `version >= 1`.\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `current_stage = 'INITIATED@' || debit_party`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, `last_memo = 'Settlement initiated'`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`, and `updated_at > created_at`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`) and a strictly increasing `updated_at` timestamp (`NEW.updated_at > OLD.updated_at`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n  - `clearledger.settlements` is an append-only ledger aggregate root (`DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with canonical trimmed storage (`col = btrim(col)`) and length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null canonically trimmed `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` canonically trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` with `data.clearingStage = 'INITIATED@' || (data.debitParty)` and `data.memo = 'Settlement initiated'` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be strictly greater (`>`) than the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`, with canonical trimmed `correlation_id` (`correlation_id = btrim(correlation_id)`).\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `attempts = 0`, both `published_at` and `last_error` must be `NULL`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; when `last_error IS NOT NULL`, `published_at IS NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `length(btrim(last_error)) > 0`, and `last_error = btrim(last_error)`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; on `UPDATE`, envelope columns are immutable, `attempts` cannot decrease, publishing an unpublished row requires incrementing `attempts` with `archived_at IS NULL`, while `published_at` remains `NOT NULL` (such as during archival or resetting `archived_at = NULL` for S3 re-archival), `published_at`, `attempts`, and `last_error` cannot be mutated (resetting `published_at = NULL, archived_at = NULL` for operational replay is permitted), and once `OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL` and `NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL`, `NEW.archived_at` cannot be mutated to a different timestamp without first resetting `archived_at = NULL`.\n\n### 4. true`\n     - `batch_size = 5`\n     - `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`\n     - `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`\n2. **Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay`)**:\n   - `image_uri = relay_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.relay_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE = \"50\"`, `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n=== scheduler\n# EventBridge Scheduler (`services/scheduler.md`)\n\nProvision two enabled EventBridge Scheduler schedules (`aws_scheduler_schedule`, `state = \"ENABLED\"`, `flexible_time_window { mode = \"OFF\" }`):\n\n1. **Outbox Relay Schedule (`schedules.outbox_schedule_name`)**:\n   - `schedule_expression = \"rate(1 minute)\"`\n   - `target.arn = workers.outbox_relay.function_arn`\n   - `target.role_arn = iam.scheduler_role_arn`\n2. events:\n\n- `billing_mode = \"PAY_PER_REQUEST\"`\n- Primary key:\n  - Partition key (`hash_key`): `PK` (`S`)\n  - Sort key (`range_key`): `SK` (`S`)\n- Global Secondary Index (`global_secondary_index`):\n  - `name = \"AccountIndex\"` (`manifest.projections.gsi_name = \"AccountIndex\"`)\n  - `hash_key = \"GSI1PK\"` (`S`)\n  - `range_key = \"GSI1SK\"` (`S`)\n  - `projection_type = \"ALL\"`\n- Point-in-time recovery (`point_in_time_recovery`):\n  - `enabled = true`\n- Server-side encryption (`server_side_encryption`):\n  - `enabled = true`\n  - `kms_key_arn = kms.projection_arn`\n\n## Projection Item Structure and Reconciliation Invariants\n\nFor each settlement `<settlement_id>`, the table stores:\n- One aggregate state item with `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, and attributes `settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `status`, `clearing_stage`, `version` (`N`), `entry_count` (`N - 1`), `updated_at` (RFC3339 timestamp of the latest event), and optional `last_entry_id` / `last_memo`.\n- Ordered ledger event items with `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"` (`EVENT#00000001` .. `EVENT#<version:08d>`) containing `settlement_id`, `event_id`, `version` (`N`), `event_type`, `status`, `clearing_stage`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, `envelope`, and optional `entry_id` / `memo`.\n\nBecause `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, `deploy.sh` must ensure full 1-to-1 convergence between PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements` and `clearledger.events`) and DynamoDB/Valkey so that no orphan partitions, stray sort keys, missing event items, or stale/divergent attributes remain in either DynamoDB or Valkey.\n\n=== elasticache-valkey\n# ElastiCache for Valkey (`services/elasticache-valkey.md`)\n\n- Provision an ElastiCache subnet group (`aws_elasticache_subnet_group`) spanning the private subnets.\n- Provision a Valkey 8 cache using `aws_elasticache_replication_group` (or `aws_elasticache_cluster`) with:\n  - `replication_group_id` (or `cluster_id`) prefixed with `<resource_prefix>`\n  - `engine = \"valkey\"` and `engine_version = \"8.0\"` (`engine = \"redis\"` is also accepted for provider compatibility)\n  - `node_type = \"cache.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `num_cache_clusters = 1` (or `num_cache_nodes = 1`)\n  - `port = 6379`\n  - `subnet_group_name` referencing the ElastiCache subnet group\n  - `security_group_ids` containing the `valkey` security group\n  - In-transit and at-rest encryption (`transit_encryption_enabled`, `at_rest_encryption_enabled`) are not required; cache connections use plain `redis://<endpoint>:<port>` inside the private VPC.\n- Pass `redis://<endpoint>:<port>` as `VALKEY_URL` to both the API service and the Projector Lambda, and set `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = \"90\"` on the API container.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, reconcile the Valkey instance against `clearledger.settlements` and `clearledger.events` so that Valkey contains **only** `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` keys for settlements present in `clearledger.settlements` (purging any orphan settlement keys as well as any stray keys outside the `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` namespace), and every settlement in `clearledger.settlements` is actively populated in Valkey at `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` with a valid TTL in `(0, 90]` and a canonical `SettlementProjection` JSON payload matching PostgreSQL (so `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` immediately after `deploy.sh` hits cache and returns `X-ClearLedger-Source: cache`).\n=== s3\n# S3 Audit Archive (`services/s3.md`)\n\n- Provision a dedicated S3 bucket (`aws_s3_bucket`) for NDJSON audit batches (`force_destroy = true` recommended so `destroy.sh` can remove the bucket after versioned audit objects and delete markers are written).\n- Enable bucket versioning (`aws_s3_bucket_versioning` with `status = \"Enabled\"`).\n- Enable default server-side encryption (`aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`) using `sse_algorithm = \"aws:kms\"` and `kms_master_key_id = kms.audit_arn`.\n- Block all public access (`aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`) with all four flags (`block_public_acls`, `block_public_policy`, `ignore_public_acls`, `restrict_public_buckets`) set to `true`.\n- Configure the Audit Archiver Lambda with `AUDIT_BUCKET = <bucket_name>` and `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"` (`manifest.audit.prefix = \"ledger-audit/\"`).\n- `clearledger-audit-archiver` writes NDJSON batches to `s3://<bucket_name>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, where `<first_seq:08d>` and `<last_seq:08d>` are the 8-digit zero-padded first and last `clearledger.outbox.seq` in the batch, each batch contains a contiguous, gap-free slice of all committed `clearledger.outbox` rows whose `seq` lies in `[first_seq, last_seq]` ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, batch `[first_seq, last_seq]` intervals are strictly disjoint and non-overlapping across objects, and `<16_char_sha256_hex>` is the first 16 lowercase hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 digest of the object's raw bytes.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, reconcile the versioned S3 audit bucket against `clearledger.outbox` so that all objects reside under `ledger-audit/`, every batch object has a valid canonical key (`batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`), valid SHA-256 digest, contiguous gap-free `clearledger.outbox.seq` coverage across `[first_seq, last_seq]`, and non-overlapping `[first_seq, last_seq]` bounds across batches, every committed event in `clearledger.outbox` appears **exactly once** across the S3 audit batches with its exact canonical JSON `payload` and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL, and all noncurrent object versions (`IsLatest = false`) and delete markers are permanently purged from the versioned bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each valid canonical batch remains.\n\n=== cognito\n# Cognito User Pool and OAuth2 Scopes (`services/vpc.md`)\n\n- Provision one VPC (`aws_vpc`) with DNS support (`enable_dns_support = true`) and DNS hostnames (`enable_dns_hostnames = true`) enabled.\n- Create at least two public subnets (`aws_subnet`, `map_public_ip_on_launch = true`) across `us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`.\n- Create at least two private subnets (`aws_subnet`, `map_public_ip_on_launch = false`) across `us-east-1a` and subnets.\n- Associate (`aws_route_table_association`) a private route table (with no route to an Internet Gateway) with both private subnets.\n- Create four distinct dedicated security groups (`aws_security_group`, four distinct security group IDs) enforcing least-privilege ingress and egress:\n  - `alb`:\n    - **Ingress**: TCP port `80` (`from_port = 80`, `to_port = 80`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from `0.0.0.0/0`.\n    - **Egress**: TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) targeting the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (must not have explicit TCP/UDP egress rules allowing `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0`).\n  - `ecs`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `alb` security group ID (never `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or any CIDR block).\n    - **Egress**: outbound traffic to the AWS control plane and data stores.\n  - `rds`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `5432` (`from_port = 5432`, `to_port = 5432`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (never `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0`).\n    - **Egress**: no explicit outbound TCP/UDP egress rules (`egress = []`; passive data stores do not initiate outbound connections).\n  - `valkey`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `6379` (`from_port = 6379`, `to_port = 6379`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (never `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0`).\n    - **Egress**: no explicit outbound TCP/UDP egress rules (`egress = []`; passive data stores do not initiate outbound connections).\n- During `deploy.sh` convergence, revoke any out-of-band public (`0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0`) ingress rules added to `ecs`, `rds`, or `valkey` and any out-of-band egress rules added to `rds` or `valkey`.\n- Tag the VPC, subnets, internet gateway, route tables, and security groups with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>`.\n\n\n=== alb\n# Application Load The first returns:\\nHTTP/1.1 400 Bad Request\\nContent-Type: application/xml\\n<?xml version=\\\"1.0\\\" encoding=\\\"UTF-8\\\"?>\\n<Error>\\n<Code>InvalidRequest</Code>\\n<Message>Unsupported resource type. the subsequent tag read fails every apply.\\n→ HTTP 200 + <ListTagsForResourceResult>\\nSuggested fix\\nIn S3ControlController.extractBucketName(), after URL-decoding, accept both:\\n* arn:aws:s3:<region>:<account>:bucket/<name> (current)\\n* arn:aws:s3:::<name> (plain S3 resource ARN — parse as arn:aws:s3:<empty>:<empty>:<name>)\\nEnvironment\\n→ HTTP 200 + <ListTagsForResourceResult>\\n* Floci: hectorvent/floci:1.5.5\\n* Terraform: 1.5.1\\n* Terraform AWS provider: hashicorp/aws v6.41.0 (Go AWS SDK v2)\\n* Real-world repo where this surfaced: tagged aws_s3_bucket resources (2/2 buckets fail apply)\\nRelated\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"url\": \"https://github.com/hashicorp/terraform-provider-aws/releases/tag/v6.23.0\",\n      \"title\": \"Release v6.23.0 · hashicorp/terraform-provider-aws · GitHub\",\n      \"publish_date\": null,\n      \"excerpts\": [\n        \"Release: v6.23.0\\n* Page: GitHub release\\n* URL: https://github.com/hashicorp/terraform-provider-aws/releases/tag/v6.23.0\\n* Repository: hashicorp/terraform-provider-aws\\n* Tag: v6.23.0\\n* Published: 2025-11-26T19:53:24Z\\n6.23.0 (November 26, 2025)\\nNOTES:\\n* resource/aws_s3_bucket: To support ABAC (Attribute Based Access Control) in general purpose buckets, this resource will now attempt to send tags in the create request and use the S3 Control tagging APIs TagResource , UntagResource , and ListTagsForResource for read and update operations.\\nThe calling principal must have the corresponding s3:TagResource , s3:UntagResource , and s3:ListTagsForResource IAM permissions . FUNCTION clearledger.cl_is_trimmed(v text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT v IS NULL OR (v = btrim(v) AND v !~ '^[[:space:]]' AND v !~ '[[:space:]]$')\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.cl_txt_ok(v text, min_len integer, max_len integer) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT v IS NOT NULL AND clearledger.cl_is_trimmed(v)\n         AND char_length(v) BETWEEN min_len AND max_len\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.cl_is_uuid(v text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT v IS NOT NULL AND v ~* '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$'\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.cl_status_rank(s text) RETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT CASE s\n    WHEN 'INITIATED'  THEN 0\n    WHEN 'VALIDATED'  THEN 1\n    WHEN 'RESERVED'   THEN 2\n    WHEN 'CLEARED'    THEN 3\n    WHEN 'SETTLED'    THEN 4\n    WHEN 'RECONCILED' THEN 5\n    ELSE NULL\n  END\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.cl_is_status(s text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT s IS NOT NULL AND s IN ('INITIATED','VALIDATED','RESERVED','CLEARED','SETTLED','RECONCILED','DISPUTED')\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.cl_transition_ok(old_status text, new_status text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT CASE\n    WHEN old_status IS NULL OR new_status IS NULL THEN false\n    WHEN NOT clearledger.cl_is_status(old_status) OR NOT clearledger.cl_is_status(new_status) THEN false\n    WHEN old_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN false\n    WHEN old_status = 'DISPUTED' THEN new_status IN ('DISPUTED', 'RECONCILED')\n    WHEN new_status = 'DISPUTED' THEN true\n    ELSE clearledger.cl_status_rank(new_status) >= clearledger.cl_status_rank(old_status)\n  END\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.cl_is_timestamp(v text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nDECLARE\n  t timestamptz;\nBEGIN\n  IF v IS NULL OR v !~ '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}[Tt ][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]+)?([Zz]|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$' THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n  t := v::timestamptz;\n  RETURN t IS NOT NULL;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.cl_json_str(p jsonb, k text) RETURNS text\nLANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\n  SELECT CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(p -> k) = 'string' THEN p ->> k ELSE NULL END\n$$;\n\n-- Strict validation of ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope (schemas/events.schema.json)\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.cl_valid_envelope(p jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nDECLARE\n  d jsonb;\n  k text;\n  v numeric;\n  ver integer;\n  etype text;\n  kind text;\n  st text;\n  stage text;\n  debit text;\n  credit text;\n  memo_t text;\n  entry_t text;\nBEGIN\n  IF p IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  -- additionalProperties: false + all required keys present\n  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(p) LOOP\n    IF k NOT IN ('schemaVersion','eventId','eventType','aggregateType','aggregateId',\n                 'aggregateVersion','occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data') THEN\n      RETURN false;\n 'aggregateVersion','occurredAt','correlationId','idempotencyKey','data']) THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF clearledger.cl_json_str(p, 'schemaVersion') IS DISTINCT FROM '1.0' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_is_uuid(clearledger.cl_json_str(p, 'eventId')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  etype := clearledger.cl_json_str(p, 'eventType');\n  IF etype IS NULL OR etype NOT IN ('SettlementInitiated', 'LedgerEntryRecorded') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF clearledger.cl_json_str(p, 'aggregateType') IS DISTINCT FROM 'settlement' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_is_uuid(clearledger.cl_json_str(p, 'aggregateId')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p -> 'aggregateVersion') <> 'number' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := (p ->> 'aggregateVersion')::numeric;\n  IF v <> trunc(v) OR v < 1 OR v > 2147483647 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  ver := v::integer;\n\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_is_timestamp(clearledger.cl_json_str(p, 'occurredAt')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_txt_ok(clearledger.cl_json_str(p, 'correlationId'), 4, 128) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_txt_ok(clearledger.cl_json_str(p, 'idempotencyKey'), 8, 128) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  d := p -> 'data';\n  IF d IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(d) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END END IF;\n  END LOOP;\n\n  kind := clearledger.cl_json_str(d, 'kind');\n  IF kind IS NULL OR kind NOT IN ('settlementInitiated', 'ledgerEntryRecorded') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_txt_ok(clearledger.cl_json_str(d, 'accountId'), 3, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_txt_ok(clearledger.cl_json_str(d, 'reference'), 3, 64) THEN RETURN false; END clearledger.cl_txt_ok(credit, 2, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF debit = credit THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  st := clearledger.cl_json_str(d, 'status');\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_is_status(st) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  stage := clearledger.cl_json_str(d, 'clearingStage');\n  IF stage IS NULL OR NOT clearledger.cl_is_trimmed(stage) OR char_length(stage) < 2 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  -- entryId: uuid string or null\n  IF d ? 'entryId' AND jsonb_typeof(d -> 'entryId') <> 'null' THEN\n    IF jsonb_typeof(d -> 'entryId') <> 'string' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    entry_t := d ->> 'entryId';\n    IF NOT clearledger.cl_is_uuid(entry_t) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  -- memo: string (1..256, trimmed) or null\n  IF d ? false; END IF;\n    IF st <> 'INITIATED' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF entry_t IS NOT NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF stage <> ('INITIATED@' || debit) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Column-to-envelope equality (events & outbox)\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.cl_envelope_matches(\n  p jsonb, p_event_id uuid, p_settlement_id uuid, p_version integer, p_correlation_id text\n) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_valid_envelope(p) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  RETURN (p ->> 'idempotencyKey') = p_idempotency_key\n     AND (p ->> 'occurredAt')::timestamptz = p_occurred_at;\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.cl_ensure_constraint(p_table text, p_name text, p_def text)\nRETURNS void LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint c\n    JOIN pg_class t ON t.oid = c.conrelid\n    JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = t.relnamespace\n    WHERE n.nspname = clearledger.cl_ensure_constraint('idempotency_keys', 'idempotency_keys_status_code_chk',\n  $c$CHECK (\n      (scope LIKE 'create:%' AND status_code = 201\n        AND jsonb_typeof(response_body -> 'version') = 'number' AND response_body ->> 'version' = '1')\n   OR (scope LIKE 'entry:%' AND status_code = 202\n        AND jsonb_typeof(response_body -> 'version') = 'number' AND response_body ->> 'version' ~ '^[0-9]+$'\n        AND (response_body ->> 'version')::numeric >= 2))$c$);\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Trigger functions\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_settlements_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.settlements is append-only: DELETE is not permitted (settlement %)', OLD.settlement_id\n      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' THEN\n    IF NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id\n       OR NEW.account_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.account_id\n       OR NEW.reference IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.reference\n       OR NEW.debit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.debit_party\n       OR NEW.credit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.credit_party\n       OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement header columns are immutable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is RECONCILED (terminal)', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.version + 1 THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement version must advance by exactly 1 (% -> %)', OLD.version, NEW.version\n        USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.entry_count IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.entry_count + 1 THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement entry_count must advance by exactly 1' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.last_entry_id IS NULL OR NEW.last_entry_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement update requires a new last_entry_id' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT (NEW.updated_at > OLD.updated_at) THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement updated_at must strictly increase' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.cl_transition_ok(OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status) THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'illegal settlement status transition % -> %', OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status\n        USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_events_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  s clearledger.settlements%ROWTYPE;\n  max_ver integer;\n  prev record;\n  d jsonb;\n  entry_t text;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP IN ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.events is append-only: % is not permitted', TG_OP USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_event_matches(NEW.payload, NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id, NEW.aggregate_version,\n                                      NEW.event_type, NEW.correlation_id, NEW.idempotency_key, NEW.occurred_at) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event payload does not conform to ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope or mismatches columns'\n      USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n  END IF;\n\n  d := NEW.payload -> 'data';\n  entry_t := CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(d -> 'entryId') = 'string' THEN d ->> 'entryId' ELSE NULL END;\n\n  SELECT * INTO s FROM clearledger.settlements WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % does not exist', NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT max(aggregate_version) INTO max_ver FROM clearledger.events WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM COALESCE(max_ver, 0) + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event aggregate_version % is not contiguous (expected %)', NEW.aggregate_version, COALESCE(max_ver, 0) + 1\n      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF entry_t IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (\n      SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n      WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id\n        AND e.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded'\n        AND lower(e.payload -> 'data' ->> 'entryId') = lower(entry_t)) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'entryId % already recorded for settlement %', entry_t, NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF s.account_id IS DISTINCT FROM d ->> 'accountId'\n     OR s.reference IS DISTINCT FROM d ->> 'reference'\n     OR s.debit_party IS DISTINCT FROM d ->> 'debitParty'\n     OR s.credit_party IS DISTINCT FROM d ->> 'creditParty'\n     OR s.current_status IS DISTINCT FROM d ->> 'status'\n     OR s.current_stage IS DISTINCT FROM d ->> 'clearingStage'\n     OR s.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM entry_t::uuid\n     OR s.last_memo IS DISTINCT FROM (CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(d -> 'memo') = 'string' THEN d ->> 'memo' ELSE NULL END)\n     OR s.version IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.aggregate_version\n     OR s.updated_at IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.occurred_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event does not match settlement % state', NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version = 1 THEN\n    IF s.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.occurred_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'initiation event occurred_at must equal settlement created_at' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    SELECT e.occurred_at, e.payload -> 'data' ->> 'status' AS status INTO prev\n      FROM clearledger.events e\n     WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND e.aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'preceding event missing' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT (NEW.occurred_at > prev.occurred_at) THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event occurred_at must be strictly after the preceding event' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.cl_transition_ok(prev.status, d ->> 'status') THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'illegal status transition % -> %', prev.status, d ->> 'status' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  e record;\n  max_ver integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.outbox rows cannot be deleted' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN\n    SELECT ev.event_id, ev.settlement_id, ev.aggregate_version, ev.correlation_id, ev.payload INTO e\n      FROM clearledger.events ev WHERE ev.event_id = NEW.event_id;\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox event % does not exist in clearledger.events', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n    END IF;\n    IF e.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.settlement_id\n       OR e.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.aggregate_version\n       OR e.correlation_id IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.correlation_id\n       OR e.payload IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.payload THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox row must mirror clearledger.events row %', NEW.event_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    SELECT max(aggregate_version) INTO max_ver FROM clearledger.outbox WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n    IF NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM COALESCE(max_ver, 0) + 1 THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox aggregate_version % is not contiguous', NEW.aggregate_version USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n\n  -- UPDATE\n  IF NEW.seq IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.seq\n     OR NEW.event_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.event_id\n     OR NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.aggregate_version\n     OR NEW.correlation_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.correlation_id\n     OR NEW.payload IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.payload\n     OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox envelope columns are immutable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.attempts < OLD.attempts THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox attempts cannot decrease' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF OLD.published_at IS NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    -- publishing\n    IF NEW.attempts <= OLD.attempts THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'publishing an outbox row requires incrementing attempts' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'cannot archive while publishing' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    IF NEW.published_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.published_at\n       OR NEW.attempts IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.attempts\n       OR NEW.last_error IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_error THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'published outbox delivery columns are immutable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL\n       AND NEW.archived_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'archived_at cannot be changed without first resetting it to NULL' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    -- operational replay reset\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'replay reset requires archived_at = NULL' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    -- unpublished -> unpublished (delivery failure bookkeeping)\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'unpublished outbox rows cannot be archived' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_idempotency_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  r jsonb;\n  ev_id uuid;\n  st_id uuid;\n  ver integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP IN ('UPDATE', 'DELETE') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.idempotency_keys is immutable: % is not permitted', TG_OP USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  r := NEW.response_body;\n  IF NOT clearledger.cl_valid_write_response(r, NEW.scope) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency response_body is not a valid WriteAcceptedResponse' USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n  END IF;\n  ev_id := (r ->> 'eventId')::uuid;\n  st_id := (r ->> 'settlementId')::uuid;\n  ver := (r ->> 'version')::integer;\n\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n                 WHERE e.event_id = ev_id AND e.settlement_id = st_id\n                   AND e.aggregate_version = ver AND e.idempotency_key = NEW.idempotency_key) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency record references unknown event %', ev_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.outbox o\n                 WHERE o.event_id = ev_id AND o.settlement_id = st_id AND o.aggregate_version = ver) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'idempotency record references event % missing from outbox', ev_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.idempotency_keys k\n             WHERE (k.response_body ->> 'eventId')::uuid = ev_id) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'eventId % already has an idempotency record', ev_id USING ERRCODE = '23505';\n  END IF;\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.idempotency_keys k\n             WHERE (k.response_body ->> 'settlementId')::uuid = st_id\n               AND (k.response_body ->> 'version')::integer = ver) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % version % already has an idempotency record', st_id, ver USING ERRCODE = '23505';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Triggers\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nCREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER trg_settlements_guard\n  BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.settlements\n  FOR EACH ROW clearledger.trg_events_guard();\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER trg_outbox_guard\n  BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.outbox\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION clearledger.trg_outbox_guard();\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER trg_idempotency_guard\n  BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON clearledger.idempotency_keys\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION ALTER TABLE clearledger.outbox ENABLE TRIGGER ALL;\n  ALTER TABLE clearledger.idempotency_keys ENABLE TRIGGER ALL;\nEXCEPTION WHEN insufficient_privilege THEN\n  NULL;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- def text;\nBEGIN\n  FOR r IN SELECT * FROM (VALUES\n    ('idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished',\n     'CREATE INDEX idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished ON clearledger.outbox USING btree (seq) WHERE Your job is to write the Terraform or OpenTofu configuration, wire up the networking, KMS keys, Cognito OAuth2 scopes, and IAM roles, initialize the PostgreSQL schema and triggers, and provide deployment and teardown scripts that hold up under live traffic and recovery drills.  The application is already compiled into four container images and loaded into the local runtime. Do not rebuild or modify the application binaries. Your work is strictly on the infrastructure, database schema, and operational lifecycle side:  1. **API service (`api_image`)**: Serves the HTTP API (`POST /v1/settlements`, `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `GET /v1/settlements/{id}`, `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`, `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`, `/health/live`, and `/health/ready`). Run this on **ECS Fargate** (`desired_count = 2` across `us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`) in private subnets behind an internet-facing **Application Load Balancer** (`port 80` HTTP listener forwarding to a target group on `port 8080` with `/health/ready` health checks). 2. **Projector (`projector_image`)**: Consumes domain events from the main **SQS** queue, writes the current settlement projection and ordered ledger event items into **DynamoDB**, and invalidates stale cached projections in **ElastiCache for Valkey**. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function connected to the main SQS queue via an event source mapping (`batch_size = 5`, `ReportBatchItemFailures` enabled) backed by a dead-letter queue (`maxReceiveCount = 4`). 3. **Outbox relay (`relay_image`)**: Polls **Amazon RDS for PostgreSQL** for committed outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`), publishes them to the main SQS queue, and stamps `published_at`. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every minute (`rate(1 minute)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**. 4. **Audit archiver (`archiver_image`)**: Reads published settlement events from PostgreSQL (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) and writes deterministic NDJSON audit batches (`ledger-audit/batch-*.ndjson`) to a private, versioned **Amazon S3** bucket. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every five minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**.  ## Workspace Environment and Contracts  The workspace comes with `terraform`, `tofu`, `aws`, `psql`, `jq`, `curl`, and Python 3 pre-installed. The local AWS control plane (`http://aws:4566`, region `us-east-1`) is configured through `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL` and `/workspace/config/config.json`. Docker is intentionally disabled inside the workspace; provision every cloud resource through Terraform or OpenTofu against the AWS endpoint.  Read the contract files under `/workspace/contracts/` before writing your code:  - `/workspace/contracts/runtime.md`: Container environment variables for the API and each Lambda worker (`DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `PROJECTION_TABLE`, `VALKEY_URL`, `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX`, `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_AUDIENCES`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`), the three non-hierarchical Cognito OAuth2 scopes (`clearledger/read`, `clearledger/write`, `clearledger/admin`), and the data-plane reconciliation rules required when `deploy.sh` runs. - `/workspace/contracts/infrastructure.md` and `/workspace/contracts/services/*.md`: Service-by-service infrastructure requirements covering:   - `services/vpc.md`: Two-AZ VPC (`us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`), public and private subnets, route tables, and four least-privilege security groups (`alb`, `ecs`, `rds`, `valkey`).   - `services/alb.md` and `services/ecs.md`: Application Load Balancer, target group, HTTP listener, ECS cluster (`containerInsights = \"enabled\"`), Fargate task definition, and service configuration.   - `services/rds.md`: RDS PostgreSQL 16 (`db.t4g.micro`) instance settings and the `clearledger` schema (`settlements`, `events`, `outbox`, `idempotency_keys`), `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, PL/pgSQL triggers, and partial/composite indexes.   - `services/sqs.md`, `services/lambda.md`, and `services/scheduler.md`: Main SQS queue and DLQ parameters, Lambda container workers, SQS event source mapping, and EventBridge Scheduler rules.   - `services/dynamodb.md`, `services/elasticache-valkey.md`, and `services/s3.md`: DynamoDB key schema (`PK`/`SK`), `AccountIndex` GSI (`GSI1PK`/`GSI1SK`), and point-in-time recovery, ElastiCache Valkey 8 (`cache.t4g.micro`), and S3 bucket versioning, KMS encryption, public-access block, and canonical batch key layout.   - `services/cognito.md`, `services/iam.md`, `services/kms.md`, and `services/cloudwatch-logs.md`: Cognito User Pool, resource server, and three client-credentials app clients, the six dedicated IAM roles (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`, `scheduler`) with both least-privilege `Allow` statements and explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` guardrails, the four customer-managed KMS keys and aliases (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and the four CloudWatch Log Groups (`retention_in_days >= 14`). - `/workspace/contracts/openapi.yaml`: HTTP endpoints, request and response bodies, required request headers (`Idempotency-Key`, `X-Correlation-Id`), and response headers (`X-ClearLedger-Source`, `X-ClearLedger-Version`, `X-ClearLedger-Instance`). - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/events.schema.json`: Domain event envelope schema used on SQS and in S3 NDJSON audit batches. - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`: JSON Schema for `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (`additionalProperties: false` throughout).  Deployment inputs (`resource_prefix`, `region`, `aws_endpoint_url`, `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`, and the four container image tags and digests: `api_image` / `clearledger/api:1.0.0`, `projector_image` / `clearledger/projector:1.0.0`, `relay_image` / `clearledger/relay:1.0.0`, `archiver_image` / `clearledger/archiver:1.0.0`) are stored in `/workspace/config/config.json`. Read those values dynamically in `deploy.sh` and `destroy.sh` instead of hardcoding values from the current workspace.  ## Deliverables and Lifecycle Rules  Write your solution under `/workspace/submission/`:  ```text /workspace/submission/ ????????? deploy.sh ????????? destroy.sh ????????? manifest.json ????????? infra/     ????????? *.tf (or *.tofu) ```  - `infra/`: Your Terraform or OpenTofu configuration. All required cloud resources and IAM policies must be declared here and tracked in `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`. Do not create scored cloud resources via imperative `aws` CLI commands in `deploy.sh`. - `deploy.sh` (timeout: 720 seconds, max combined stdout/stderr: 8 MiB):   - Initializes and applies `/workspace/submission/infra` with local state at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.   - Idempotently creates and enables the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema, tables, relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, triggers, and indexes on the RDS instance as specified in `/workspace/contracts/services/rds.md`.   - Exports `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (max 1 MiB) from your Terraform/OpenTofu outputs matching `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`.   - Waits until `GET <service_url>/health/ready` returns HTTP `200`.   - Handles repair and re-apply cleanly: re-running `deploy.sh` must preserve the existing RDS instance, DynamoDB table, and S3 bucket without losing committed data, repair out-of-band control-plane drift and recreate any deleted resources across all services (`/workspace/contracts/services/*.md`), and converge the outbox, DynamoDB projections, Valkey cache, and versioned S3 `ledger-audit/` archive 1-to-1 against PostgreSQL before exiting `0`. - `destroy.sh` (timeout: 900 seconds, max combined stdout/stderr: 8 MiB):   - Tears down all resources created for the active `resource_prefix` while leaving pre-existing baseline (`cl-base-*`) resources untouched.   - Cleans up any out-of-band operational resources or policy attachments scoped to `<resource_prefix>` so `terraform.tfstate` has zero remaining managed resources and post-destroy cloud inventory matches the pre-deployment baseline. The application is already compiled into four container images and loaded into the local runtime. Do not rebuild or modify the application binaries. Your work is strictly on the infrastructure, database schema, and operational lifecycle side:  1. **API service (`api_image`)**: Serves the HTTP API (`POST /v1/settlements`, `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `GET /v1/settlements/{id}`, `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`, `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`, `/health/live`, and `/health/ready`). Run this on **ECS Fargate** (`desired_count = 2` across `us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`) in private subnets behind an internet-facing **Application Load Balancer** (`port 80` HTTP listener forwarding to a target group on `port 8080` with `/health/ready` health checks). 2. **Projector (`projector_image`)**: Consumes domain events from the main **SQS** queue, writes the current settlement projection and ordered ledger event items into **DynamoDB**, and invalidates stale cached projections in **ElastiCache for Valkey**. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function connected to the main SQS queue via an event source mapping (`batch_size = 5`, `ReportBatchItemFailures` enabled) backed by a dead-letter queue (`maxReceiveCount = 4`). 3. **Outbox relay (`relay_image`)**: Polls **Amazon RDS for PostgreSQL** for committed outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`), publishes them to the main SQS queue, and stamps `published_at`. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every minute (`rate(1 minute)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**. 4. **Audit archiver (`archiver_image`)**: Reads published settlement events from PostgreSQL (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) and writes deterministic NDJSON audit batches (`ledger-audit/batch-*.ndjson`) to a private, versioned **Amazon S3** bucket. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every five minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**.  ## Workspace Environment and Contracts  The workspace comes with `terraform`, `tofu`, `aws`, `psql`, `jq`, `curl`, and Python 3 pre-installed. The local AWS control plane (`http://aws:4566`, region `us-east-1`) is configured through `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL` and `/workspace/config/config.json`. Docker is intentionally disabled inside the workspace; provision every cloud resource through Terraform or OpenTofu against the AWS endpoint.  Read the contract files under `/workspace/contracts/` before writing your code:  - `/workspace/contracts/runtime.md`: Container environment variables for the API and each Lambda worker (`DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `PROJECTION_TABLE`, `VALKEY_URL`, `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX`, `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_AUDIENCES`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`), the three non-hierarchical Cognito OAuth2 scopes (`clearledger/read`, `clearledger/write`, `clearledger/admin`), and the data-plane reconciliation rules required when `deploy.sh` runs. - `/workspace/contracts/infrastructure.md` and `/workspace/contracts/services/*.md`: Service-by-service infrastructure requirements covering:   - `services/vpc.md`: Two-AZ VPC (`us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`), public and private subnets, route tables, and four least-privilege security groups (`alb`, `ecs`, `rds`, `valkey`).   - `services/alb.md` and `services/ecs.md`: Application Load Balancer, target group, HTTP listener, ECS cluster (`containerInsights = \"enabled\"`), Fargate task definition, and service configuration.   - `services/rds.md`: RDS PostgreSQL 16 (`db.t4g.micro`) instance settings and the `clearledger` schema (`settlements`, `events`, `outbox`, `idempotency_keys`), `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, PL/pgSQL triggers, and partial/composite indexes.   - `services/sqs.md`, `services/lambda.md`, and `services/scheduler.md`: Main SQS queue and DLQ parameters, Lambda container workers, SQS event source mapping, and EventBridge Scheduler rules.   - `services/dynamodb.md`, `services/elasticache-valkey.md`, and `services/s3.md`: DynamoDB key schema (`PK`/`SK`), `AccountIndex` GSI (`GSI1PK`/`GSI1SK`), and point-in-time recovery, ElastiCache Valkey 8 (`cache.t4g.micro`), and S3 bucket versioning, KMS encryption, public-access block, and canonical batch key layout.   - `services/cognito.md`, `services/iam.md`, `services/kms.md`, and `services/cloudwatch-logs.md`: Cognito User Pool, resource server, and three client-credentials app clients, the six dedicated IAM roles (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`, `scheduler`) with both least-privilege `Allow` statements and explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` guardrails, the four customer-managed KMS keys and aliases (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and the four CloudWatch Log Groups (`retention_in_days >= 14`). - `/workspace/contracts/openapi.yaml`: HTTP endpoints, request and response bodies, required request headers (`Idempotency-Key`, `X-Correlation-Id`), and response headers (`X-ClearLedger-Source`, `X-ClearLedger-Version`, `X-ClearLedger-Instance`). - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/events.schema.json`: Domain event envelope schema used on SQS and in S3 NDJSON audit batches. - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`: JSON Schema for `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (`additionalProperties: false` throughout).  Deployment inputs (`resource_prefix`, `region`, `aws_endpoint_url`, `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`, and the four container image tags and digests: `api_image` / `clearledger/api:1.0.0`, `projector_image` / `clearledger/projector:1.0.0`, `relay_image` / `clearledger/relay:1.0.0`, `archiver_image` / `clearledger/archiver:1.0.0`) are stored in `/workspace/config/config.json`. Read those values dynamically in `deploy.sh` and `destroy.sh` instead of hardcoding values from the current workspace.  ## Deliverables and Lifecycle Rules  Write your solution under `/workspace/submission/`:  ```text /workspace/submission/ ????????? deploy.sh ????????? destroy.sh ????????? manifest.json ????????? infra/     ????????? *.tf (or *.tofu) ```  - `infra/`: Your Terraform or OpenTofu configuration.
